# Third-Party Notices

This generated inventory records the package versions and license evidence listed below.
Generation uses exact release artifacts and, when supplied, exact public package payloads selected by tracked Packman pins.
Do not edit it by hand. Coverage is limited to the listed records from selected public release payloads. Aggregate Kit records preserve packaged third-party notice bundles when individual embedded component versions are unavailable.
Full license texts are tracked in `licenses/`; packaged releases include the equivalent assembled `ThirdPartyNotices.txt`.
Surfaces list the platform or public package followed by `:` and its relative evidence path. Archive members follow `!`; `kit-native` paths identify the dependency manifest, not a binary location.
